                                                                Annual Report 2019/2020 年度報告      5
Chairman’s
Statement
主席報告
Whilst Vitasoy Group has faced unprecedented
external challenges during FY2019/2020, we
remain confident about our long term prospects
based on our company strengths, and stay
committed to our mission to provide tasty and
nutritious plant-based food and beverages
and doing so by procuring the best practices in
sustainability production.

維他奶集團於二零一九╱二零二零財政年度面對前
所未有的外來挑戰，但憑藉公司的雄厚實力，我們
對長遠前景仍然充滿信心，而本集團亦會繼續履行
使命，以可持續生產的方式，致力為大家帶來以植
物成分為主、既美味又營養豐富的食品及飲品。

    terms, generating RMB4,025 million in revenue. Operating profit       算銷售仍能增長 1%，錄得人民幣 4,025,000,000
    dropped 24% in local currency compared to the previous year,          元的收入。經營溢利與去年相比以當地貨幣計算
    due to a combination of declining revenue growth and of the fact      則下跌 24%，此乃由於收入增長幅度下降加上於
    that a significant portion of operating expenses have already         農曆新年期間已經支出龐大經營費用。我們於所
    been incurred during Chinese New Year. We have grown market           有銷售地區均擴大了市場份額。廣東省東莞市的
    share in all the regions where we compete, and have progressed        常平新廠房按進度興建，並可於二零二零年內開

    Guangdong province such that we can begin production in 2020.

    affected not only on-premise and on-the-go channels, but also our     情不但影響食肆及便利店等銷售渠道的表現，學
    Vitaland school tuckshop business due to school closures.             校停課亦影響了維他天地的學校小賣部業務。

    the impact of droughts, bushfires and, most recently, COVID-19.       的影響。

    and experience are ready to support a progressive return to           2019冠狀病毒病疫情逐漸消退，本集團可憑藉雄
    growth as COVID-19 conditions gradually subside. As there might       厚實力與豐富經驗，一步一步重上增長軌道。為
    be a possible second wave returning, we will cautiously pace          準備應付可能爆發第二波的疫情，我們會繼續謹

Appreciation                                                            致謝
We hope that we will be able to celebrate our 80th Anniversary in       本集團希望能於本年稍後，慶祝維他奶創立八十
the later part of this year. Meanwhile I take this opportunity to say   週年。同時，本人在此謹向董事會、管理團隊以
a big thank you to our Board of Directors, the management team          及全體員工致以衷誠謝意。全賴大家過去多年堅
and our staff. Their unwavering support, dedication and invaluable      定不移的支持，恪守職務以及付出寶貴的貢獻，
contribution has been the foundation that secured our progress          過去八十載我們才得以邁步向前。本人亦謹此對
for the past eight decades. I thank the utmost our shareholders,        股東、客戶、供應商及業務夥伴一直以來的支持
customers, suppliers and business partners for their continued          致以由衷謝意。
support.

Officer’s Report/
Business Review
集團行政總裁報告╱
業務回顧
Following consistent growth throughout the
last decade, growth continued in the first half of
FY2019/2020, but the second half of the fiscal
year saw the COVID-19 pandemic exacerbating
numerous other disruptions across the Group’s
operating units. In this context, our focus on
operational efficiencies and the protection of
our associates and assets have mitigated the
immediate impact on growth and profits, and
secured the conditions and internal readiness
to restart our growth trajectory when pandemic
restrictions subside.

於二零一九╱二零二零財政年度上半年，我們延續
過去十年的穩定增長，但於財政年度下半年爆發
2019 冠狀病毒病疫情，令本集團本經受不同問題困
擾的營運市場雪上加霜。在這個形勢之下，我們因
能致力於經營效率並保護好員工與資產，故紓緩了
增長及溢利所受的即時影響，並為在疫情限制放寬
後重返增長軌道營造了條件兼且作好內部準備。

     Financial Risk Management                                             財務風險管理
     The Group’s overall financial management policy focuses on            本集團的整體財務管理政策強調預測及管控風
     anticipating, controlling and managing risks, covering transactions   險，涵蓋與本集團的相關業務直接有關的交易。
     directly related to the underlying businesses of the Group. For       為達致協同效益、效率及監控的目的，本集團為
     synergy, efficiency and control, the Group operates a central cash    所有附屬公司實行中央現金及財政管理系統。各
     and treasury management system for all subsidiaries. Borrowings       營運附屬公司一般以當地貨幣進行借貸，為當地
     are normally taken out in local currencies by the operating           投資項目提供資金及減低部份外匯風險。
     subsidiaries to fund their investments and partially mitigate
     foreign currency risks.


     Potential Risk and Uncertainties                                      潛在風險及不明朗因素
     During the year, the Group Internal Audit and Risk Management         年內，本集團內部審計及風險管理部實施一套全
     Department implemented an all-round Risk Assessment Process           面風險評估程序，讓更多集團旗下各公司的持份

Board Diversity                                                       董事會成員多元化
The Board has adopted a Board Diversity Policy to set out the         董事會於二零一三年六月採納董事會成員多元化
objectives and the factors to be considered for achieving the         政策，當中載列董事會成員多元化的目的以及達
diversity of the Board in June 2013. The Board Diversity Policy       致多元化的考慮因素。董事會成員多元化政策已
has been published on the Company’s website.                          刊登於本公司網站。


The Company recognises and embraces the benefits of having a          本公司認同董事會成員多元化之裨益，並認為可
diverse Board to enhance the quality of its performance. Board        增強董事會之表現質素。董事之委任及續聘過往
appointment and renewal have been, and will continue to be,           根據並將繼續按候選人之功績以及能否補替及加
made based on merit as well as complementing and expanding            強董事會整體之技能、知識及經驗為準則。薪酬
the skills, knowledge and experience of the Board as a whole. The     及提名委員會將會每年檢討達致性別及年齡多元
Remuneration and Nomination Committee reviews the progress            化的可計量目標之進度並適時地檢討該等目標，
on achieving measurable objectives for gender and age diversity       以確保政策能利用多元性協助達致本公司之策略
annually and reviews such objectives, whenever applicable to          目標。薪酬及提名委員會已制定截至二零二一╱
ensure it leverages diversity to contribute to the achievement        二零二二年度達致年齡及性別多元化的可計量目
of the Company’s strategic objectives. The Remuneration               標。於截至二零二零年三月三十一日止年度內，
and Nomination Committee has established the measurable               達致該等目標之進度如下：
objectives for achieving age and gender diversity up to the year

Board Proceedings                                                         董事會程序
The Board met five times in FY2019/2020 to discuss and approve            董事會於二零一九╱二零二零財政年度內召開五
the Group’s long term strategic plan; to review and monitor the           次會議，討論及批准本集團之長期策略規劃；審
financial and business performance of the Group and each of               視及監察本集團及各營運單位之財務及業務表
its operating units; to consider and approve annual budget, the           現；考慮及審批年度預算及財務報告；審閱及批
financial reports; to review and approve sustainability report and        准可持續發展報告及與企業管治及可持續發展相
various corporate governance and sustainability related policies          關之各種政策及常規；並監察及審視本集團風險
and practices; and to monitor and review the effectiveness of the         管理表現之有效性。
Group’s risk management performance.

The Board and its Committees are supplied with full and timely            董事會及其委員會均適時獲得本公司所有相關資
information in relation to the Company, which enables them to             料，讓其能履行職責。本公司已有既定程序讓董
discharge their responsibilities. There are established procedures        事在適當的情況下，為履行其職責及責任而諮詢
for Directors to seek independent professional advice for them to         獨立專業意見，費用由本公司支付。
discharge their duties and responsibilities, where appropriate, at
the Company’s expenses.

During the year, a new ESG (Environmental, Social and                     年內，本公司新成立環境、社會及管治委員會，
Governance) Committee was set up with the main objectives to              主要目的是就本公司在集團層面的可持續發展目
provide vision, long term guidance and report to the Board on             標、策略及優先處理事項為董事會提供願景、長
the Company’s Group-level Sustainability goals, strategies and            期指引及匯報，就具重大策略意義之環境及社會
priorities and to advise the Board on environmental and social            風險向董事會提供意見，以及其預防及減輕風險
risks of strategic significance and to provide anticipatory and           計劃。
mitigation plans.

Board Committees meetings and the Annual General Meeting                  會議及股東週年大會。
during the year.

     The Board has approved a Whistleblowing Policy in March 2012        於二零一二年三月，董事會已批准採納舉報政
     which has been revised in November 2017. The main objective         策，並於二零一七年十一月作出修訂。政策之主
     of the Policy is to provide employees and external parties an       要目標是為僱員及外間人士提供一個公開及統一
     open and unified reporting channel and procedures to report         之舉報渠道及程序，舉報任何涉及本公司及其僱
     any serious misconduct or malpractice involving the Company         員之嚴重失當或不良行為。審核委員會獲委派全
     and its employees. The Audit Committee has been delegated           權負責監察及檢討舉報政策之成效。年內，概無
     with the overall responsibility for monitoring and reviewing the    從僱員或外間人士接獲涉及嚴重欺詐或失職之事
     effectiveness of the Whistleblowing Policy. During the year, no     件而對或將對本公司之財務狀況及整體營運造成
     incident involving serious fraud or misconduct have been received   重大影響。
     from employees nor external parties which had or would have a
     material impact on the Company’s financial position and overall
     operations.

     The Board has adopted a Corporate Governance Policy to provide      董事會已採納企業管治政策，為本公司在制定政
     a basic framework for developing the Company’s policies and         策及常規方面建立基本框架，確保能符合各股
     practices to ensure alignment of interests and expectations from    東、公眾投資者及其他持份者的利益與期望。企
     our shareholders, the investing public and other stakeholders.      業管治政策已刊登於本公司網站。
     The Corporate Governance Policy has been published on the
     Company’s website.

     compliance fulfillment on an ongoing basis.

     Board is now able to assess ESG risks and provide strategic long-            會及管治風險，並為可持續發展表現、目標及優
     term guidance on sustainability performance, goals and priorities.           先處理事項提供策略性長遠指導。

Evaluation of the Board and the Executive                             董事會及執行主席之評核
Chairman
The Board believes that evaluation of the Board and the Executive     董事會相信，對董事會及執行主席進行評核，乃
Chairman is an essential requirement of good corporate                良好企業管治之重要要求，並早於二零一一╱二
governance and has already adopted the Recommended Best               零一二財政年度開始採納企業管治守則中之建議
Practice for regular Board Evaluation under the CG Code as early      最佳常規定期為董事會作出評核。
in FY2011/2012.

identifying and addressing its strengths and weaknesses and           對其不足之處提出針對方案，以及突出董事會對
highlight the Board’s expectations for itself and areas for further   自身之期望及有待進一步改善之範疇。本公司亦
improvement. The Executive Chairman has also been evaluated           就執行主席作為董事會主席是否充分及有效地執
on whether he has adequately and effectively performed his roles      行其角色及履行其職責進行評核。董事會評核一
and fulfilled his responsibilities as the Chairman of the Board.      般每年進行一次。
Board evaluation is usually conducted on an annual basis.

A formal evaluation of the Board and the Executive Chairman           本公司已為董事會及執行主席進行二零一九╱二
was conducted for FY2019/2020. During the evaluation process,         零二零財政年度的正式評核。於評核過程中，本
questionnaire with scoring was used for collecting scores as well     公司採用計分問卷方式向全體董事收集評分及意
as opinions from all the Directors. The questionnaire included 20     見，當中包括 20 項評核董事會之陳述及 10 項評
statements for Board evaluation and 10 statements for Executive       核執行主席之陳述。填妥之評核表格以保密形式
Chairman evaluation. Completed evaluation forms were sent to          遞交予公司秘書。公司秘書其後就綜合評核結果
the Company Secretary on a confidential basis. The Company            編製摘要報告，並呈交於董事會會議上審閱及討
Secretary then prepared a summary report of the aggregate             論。

     The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management      董事會明白其肩負風險管理及內部監控系統之責
     and internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness.        任，並檢討其成效。該系統之設計旨在管理而非
     The systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate               消除無法達成業務目標之風險，並僅可為避免發
     the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and can only       生重大錯誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕對之保
     provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against material         證。為實現此目標，董事會確保已制定有效之風
     misstatement or loss. To achieve this, the Board ensures that          險管治架構及持續風險管理程序，以達致本公司
     there is an effective framework of risk governance and ongoing         業務之長久成功。
     risk management process to promote the long-term success of the
     Company.

     The Board is entrusted with the overall responsibility on an ongoing   董事會一直獲委以確保維持適當及有效之風險管
     basis for ensuring that appropriate and effective risk management      理及內部監控之整體職責，從而提供合理保證，
     and internal controls are maintained to provide reasonable             以避免發生資料之重大錯誤陳述。風險管理及內
     assurance against material misstatement of information. Main           部監控系統之主要特點如下 ︰
     features of the risk management and internal control systems are

controls is a continuing process in the Company. Our internal                                     公司之內部監控系統旨在合理保證：
control system is designed to give reasonable assurance that:

          or the Audit Committee.                                              進行特定審閱。


     Group Internal Audit, reporting to the Audit Committee, provides     集團內部審計向審核委員會報告，就業務營運風

     the Company structure, management’s concern, nature and              計劃時採用風險評估方法，並考慮本公司架構、
     complexity of operation, and operating environment, Group            管理層的關注範疇、營運性質及複雜性以及經營
     Internal Audit prepares its annual audit plan which is reviewed      環境。風險評估方法有助識別業務風險及釐定審
     and approved by the Audit Committee. The risk assessment             計之頻率。集團內部審計按照已批核的審計計劃
     methodology assists in identifying business risks and determining    安排內部審核程序，就財務、業務及職能方面之
     audit frequencies. Based on the approved audit plan, Group           各項營運及活動進行獨立檢討，並安排將其資源
     Internal Audit schedules its internal audit programs to conduct an   集中處理高風險的範疇。
     independent review of different financial, business and functional

     higher risk.

Risk Management Process                                                  風險管理程序
Our risk management process is embedded in our daily operations,         我們之風險管理程序已納入日常營運範圍、財務
financial activities, compliance, strategy development & business        活動、合規、策略發展與業務規劃、投資決定及
planning, investment decision and external factors. The processes        外界因素，程序如下 ︰
are as follows:

•    Anticipate: Potential risks and threats that are related to         •   預測： 所有員工均參與識別不同業務流程
     different business processes and relevant to the Company’s              及與本公司目標相關之潛在風險及威脅。
     objectives will be anticipated and identified by all individual         為便於進行適當評估，我們將風險分為六
     staff. Risks are grouped into six clusters (including reputation,       個範疇（包括信譽、策略、市場、流動資
     strategy, market, liquidity, credit and operation) to facilitate        金、信貸及營運）。
     the assessment appropriately.

•    Rate: Each identified risk is evaluated on the basis of             •   評估： 按照董事會設定之風險承受能力，
     impact and likelihood consistent with the risk appetite set             評估每項經識別風險的影響及可能性。風
     by the Board. The risk impact and likelihood will be rated              險之影響及可能性將根據過往記錄、統計
     according to the historical records, statistical analyses               分析、相關經驗及管理層之專業判斷進行
     and relevant experiences, specialist and expert judgments               評級。集團風險管理促進評估流程，包括
     by management. Group Risk Management facilitates the                    協助企業風險管理執行委員會及審核委員
     evaluation process including the summary discussion in the              會之討論。
     Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee and
     Audit Committee.

•    Respond: A delegated management is assigned to each risk            •   控制： 各項風險將配對專責管理人員，成
     as risk owner to fully respond on each risk. Risk management            為風險負責人，以有效管理風險。風險負
     strategies and corresponding control owners are identified              責人制定風險管理策略以及指派監控負責
     by risk owner to deploy appropriate control activities.                 人，實施適當監控活動。


•    Regulate: We take a close monitoring on the current status          •   監察： 我們密切監控風險現況。各風險現
     of risk, which is being monitored through key risk indicators           況的結果取決於關鍵風險指標的預設量度
     with defined thresholds, occurrence of related incidents,               級別基準、所發生之相關事故、內部監控
     control effectiveness and audit results. Any change in                  程序之成效以及審計結果。風險現況倘出
     current status of risk to a worsened level will trigger a react         現任何不良轉變，均會觸發反應程序，展
     process through creation of a mitigation plan.                          開減輕風險計劃。


•    React: When there is a change on the current status of risk,        •   緩解： 當風險現況有所改變，相應負責人
     the corresponding owner will coordinate mitigation plan                 將協調減輕風險計劃之行動細節、負責人
     with action details, responsible person and timeline to                 及時間表，以處理風險情況。
     address the status.

     The Board has adopted a Dividend Policy to provide guidance to        董事會已採納股息政策，就派發盈餘資金予股東
     the Board and the management of the Company for distribution          向董事會及管理層提供指引。
     of surplus funds to its shareholders.

     In determining the level of dividend payment, the following           就決定所派付的股息水平，須計及下列準則及考
     criteria and consideration must be taken:                             慮因素：


     –    Sharing of success with shareholders                             –   與股東共享成果
     –    Liquidity and capital requirement                                –   流動資金及資本要求
     –    Historical trend of dividend payout                              –   過往股息派付之趨勢
     –    Benchmarking with competitors within the industry                –   與業內競爭對手之標準比較
     –    Fulfilment of financial covenants                                –   符合財務契約
     –    Taxation impact                                                  –   稅務影響
     –    Restrictions                                                     –   限制條件
     –    Other factors that may deem relevant                             –   任何其他視為相關之因素


     The Company intends to declare dividends to shareholders semi-        本公司計劃每半年向股東宣派股息，每年宣派之
     annually in an aggregate amount per year of approximately 60%         股息總額預計為扣除一次性事件影響後之股東預
     of the anticipated consolidated net annual profit attributable        期應佔年度綜合溢利約 60%，並可能除中期或末
     to shareholders net of one-off event impact and may declare           期股息外不時宣派特別股息。董事會將不時酌情
     special dividends from time to time in addition to interim or         決定是否派發股息以及所派發股息之水平。
     final dividends. The determination to pay dividends and level of
     dividend to be distributed will be subject to the discretion of the
     Board from time to time.

We have audited the consolidated financial statements of                  本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於第

and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including a

accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional              則」），我們獨立於    貴集團，並已履行守則中的

                                                        務期間確認；及

      (a) Statement of compliance                                            (a) 遵例聲明
      These financial statements have been prepared in accordance            本財務報表是按照所有適用之《香港財務報告準

      (“HKFRSs”), which collective term includes all applicable individual   定而編製。《香港財務報告準則》一詞包括香港
      Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards, Hong Kong Accounting          會計師公會頒佈之所有適用之個別《香港財務報
      Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations issued by the Hong Kong        告準則》、《香港會計準則》及詮釋。本財務報表
      Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”), accounting       亦符合香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則
      principles generally accepted in Hong Kong and the requirements        （「上市規則」）之適用披露規定。本集團採納之
      of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance. These financial                  主要會計政策於下文披露。
      statements also comply with the applicable disclosure provisions
      of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock
      Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing rules”). Significant
      accounting policies adopted by the Group are disclosed below.

      The HKICPA has issued certain new and revised HKFRSs that              香港會計師公會已頒佈若干新訂及經修訂之《香
      are first effective or available for early adoption for the current    港財務報告準則》，並於本集團及本公司之當前
      accounting period of the Group and the Company. Note 1(c)              會計期間首次生效或可供提早採納。首次應用此
      provides information on any changes in accounting policies             等適用於本集團之新訂準則所引致於當前及過往
      resulting from initial application of these developments to the        會計期間之任何會計政策變動已於本財務報表內
      extent that they are relevant to the Group for the current and         反映，有關資料載於附註 1(c)。
      prior accounting periods reflected in these financial statements.

1     Significant accounting policies (Continued)                       1   主要會計政策（續）
(b) Basis of preparation of the financial statements                    (b) 財務報表之編製基準（續）
      (Continued)
The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on             儘管本集團於匯報日錄得流動負債淨額
a going concern basis notwithstanding that the Group had net            185,794,000 元，本綜合財務報表乃按持續經營
current liabilities of $185,794,000 at the end of the reporting         基準編製。於編製該等綜合財務報表時，董事已
period. In preparing these consolidated financial statements, the       審慎考慮本集團之當前及預測未來的流動資金。
Directors have given careful consideration to the current and           經計及（其中包括）(i) 於二零二零年三月三十一日
anticipated future liquidity of the Group. Taking into account, inter   之現金及銀行存款 848,275,000 元；(ii) 於匯報日
alia, (i) cash and bank deposits of $848,275,000 at 31st March          未動用之貸款授信；及 (iii) 本集團未來十二個月
2020, (ii) the unutilised loan facilities at the end of the reporting   經營業務所產生之預期現金流入淨額後，董事認
period, and (iii) the expected net cash inflows generated from the      為本集團將有能力應付其到期的債務。因此，董
Group’s operations for the next twelve months, the Directors are        事認為按持續經營基準編製該等綜合財務報表乃
of the opinion that the Group will be able to meet its liabilities as   屬恰當。
and when they fall due. Accordingly, the Directors consider that
the preparation of these consolidated financial statements on a

(i)   New definition of a lease                                          (i)   租賃之新定義

concept of control. HKFRS 16 defines a lease on the basis of             務報告準則》第 16 號根據客戶是否於一段時間內

period of time, which may be determined by a defined amount of           用量釐定。在客戶既有權指示使用已識別資產，
use. Control is conveyed where the customer has both the right to        亦有權從該用途獲得幾乎所有經濟利益的情況
direct the use of the identified asset and to obtain substantially all   下，則控制權已轉移。
of the economic benefits from that use.

      (d) Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                          (d) 附屬公司及非控股權益
      Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The Group            附屬公司為本集團所控制之實體。當本集團從參
      controls an entity when it is exposed, or has rights, to variable       與某實體之業務獲得或有權獲得可變回報，及有
      returns from its involvement with the entity and has the ability        能力藉對實體行使其權力而影響該等回報，則本
      to affect those returns through its power over the entity. When         集團控制該實體。當評估本集團是否有權力時，
      assessing whether the Group has power, only substantive rights          只考慮具體權利（由本集團及其他人士持有）。
      (held by the Group and other parties) are considered.

      An investment in a subsidiary is consolidated into the consolidated     於附屬公司之投資由該控制權生效日期起至結束
      financial statements from the date that control commences until         日期止期間於綜合財務報表綜合入賬。集團內公
      the date that control ceases. Intra-group balances, transactions        司之間之結餘、交易及現金流量，以及集團內公
      and cash flows and any unrealised profits arising from intra-group      司之間之交易所產生之任何未變現溢利，均於編
      transactions are eliminated in full in preparing the consolidated       製綜合財務報表時全數撇銷。如無減值證據，集
      financial statements. Unrealised losses resulting from intra-group      團內公司之間之交易產生之未變現虧損按未變現
      transactions are eliminated in the same way as unrealised gains         收益相同之方式撇銷。
      but only to the extent that there is no evidence of impairment.

      Non-controlling interests represent the equity in a subsidiary not      非控股權益指非本公司直接或間接應佔之附屬公
      attributable directly or indirectly to the Company, and in respect      司股權，而本集團並未與有關權益持有人協定任
      of which the Group has not agreed any additional terms with the         何附加條款，令本集團整體對該等權益產生符合
      holders of those interests which would result in the Group as a         金融負債定義之合約義務。就各企業合併而言，
      whole having a contractual obligation in respect of those interests     本集團可選擇按公允值或非控股權益所佔附屬公
      that meets the definition of a financial liability. For each business   司之可識別資產淨值之比例計量任何非控股權
      combination, the Group can elect to measure any non-controlling         益。
      interests either at fair value or at the non-controlling interests’
      proportionate share of the subsidiary’s net identifiable assets.

      (e) Joint venture                                                       (e) 合營公司
      A joint venture is an arrangement whereby the Group or Company          合營公司為本集團或本公司及其他人士按合約同
      and other parties contractually agree to share control of the           意下分配控制權及淨資產擁有權之安排。
      arrangement, and have rights to the net assets of the arrangement.

      An investment in a joint venture is accounted for in the                除合營公司的投資被分類為持作出售或納入被分
      consolidated financial statements under the equity method,              類為持作出售的出售組別外，於合營公司的投資
      unless it is classified as held for sale (or included in a disposal     是按權益法記入綜合財務報表，並且先以成本入
      group that is classified as held for sale). Under the equity method,    賬，另調整本集團於收購日應佔該投資的可辨別
      the investment is initially recorded at cost, adjusted for any excess   淨資產之公允值所超出成本之任何金額（如有）。
      of the Group’s share of the acquisition-date fair values of the         投資成本包括購買價格、直接歸屬於收購投資之
      investee’s identifiable net assets over the cost of the investment      其他成本及對構成本集團股權投資一部分之合營
      (if any). The cost of the investment includes purchase price, other     公司之任何直接投資。往後，需調整在收購後本
      costs directly attributable to the acquisition of the investment,       集團應佔該投資淨資產之變動及在附註 1(f) 及 (k)
      and any direct investment into the joint venture that forms part        (ii) 所載有關該投資的減值損失。任何超出收購日
      of the Group’s equity investment. Thereafter, the investment is         之成本、本集團應佔該投資收購後和已除稅的業
      adjusted for the post acquisition change in the Group’s share of        績及年度內的任何減值損失均在綜合損益表內確
      the investee’s net assets and any impairment loss relating to the       認，而本集團應佔該投資收購後和已除稅之其他
      investment (see notes 1(f) and (k)(ii)). Any acquisition-date excess    全面收益項目則在綜合損益及其他全面收益表內
      over cost, the Group’s share of the post-acquisition, post-tax          確認。
      results of the investees and any impairment losses for the year
      are recognised in the consolidated statement of profit or loss,
      whereas the Group’s share of the post-acquisition post-tax items
      of the investees’ other comprehensive income is recognised in the
      consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive
      income.

      When the Group’s share of losses exceeds its interest in the joint      當本集團對合營公司承擔的虧損額超過其所佔權
      venture, the Group’s interest is reduced to nil and recognition of      益時，本集團所佔權益便會減少至零，並且不再
      further losses is discontinued except to the extent that the Group      確認額外虧損；但如本集團須履行法定或推定義
      has incurred legal or constructive obligations or made payments         務，或代該投資作出付款則除外。就此而言，本
      on behalf of the investee. For this purpose, the Group’s interest is    集團所佔權益是以按照權益法計算投資的賬面金
      the carrying amount of the investment under the equity method           額，以及實質上構成本集團在合營公司投資淨額
      together with the Group’s long-term interests that in substance         一部分的長期權益。
      form part of the Group’s net investment in the joint venture.

      Unrealised profits and losses resulting from transactions between       本集團及其合營公司之間交易所產生的未變現損
      the Group and its joint venture are eliminated to the extent of the     益，均按本集團於該投資所佔的權益比率抵銷；
      Group’s interest in the investee, except where unrealised losses        但如有未變現虧損證明已轉讓資產出現減值，則
      provide evidence of an impairment of the asset transferred, in          會即時在損益中確認。
      which case they are recognised immediately in profit or loss.

If an investment in a joint venture becomes an investment in            如於一間合營公司之投資轉變為於一間聯營公司
an associate or vice versa, retained interest is not remeasured.        之投資或反之亦然，其保留的權益不用重新計
Instead, the investment continues to be accounted for under the         量，而該投資將繼續採用權益法入賬。
equity method.

In all other cases, when the Group ceases to have joint control         在其他情況，當本集團喪失對合營公司之共同控
over a joint venture, it is accounted for as a disposal of the entire   制權，將按出售該投資之所有權益入賬，而所產
interest in that investee, with a resulting gain or loss being          生的盈虧確認於損益內。在喪失共同控制權時，
recognised in profit or loss. Any interest retained in that former      保留於前度投資的任何權益按公允值確認，而此
investee at the date when joint control is lost is recognised at        金額被視為首次確認為金融資產的公允值。
fair value and this amount is regarded as the fair value on initial
recognition of a financial asset.

(f) Goodwill                                                            (f)    商譽
Goodwill represents the excess of:                                      商譽指：


(i)    the aggregate of the fair value of the consideration             (i)    已轉讓代價之公允值、於被收購方任何非
       transferred, the amount of any non-controlling interest in              控股權益金額與本集團先前持有被收購方
       the acquiree and the fair value of the Group’s previously               股本權益公允值之總和；超出
       held equity interest in the acquiree; over

(ii)   the net fair value of the acquiree’s identifiable assets and     (ii)   於收購日期計量之被收購方可識別資產及
       liabilities measured as at the acquisition date.                        負債之公允值淨額之部份。


When (ii) is greater than (i), then this excess is recognised           倘第 (ii) 項高於第 (i) 項，該差額即時於損益確認
immediately in profit or loss as a gain on a bargain purchase.          為議價收購之收益。


Goodwill is stated at cost less accumulated impairment losses.          商譽按成本值減去累計減值虧損後列賬。企業合
Goodwill arising on a business combination is allocated to each         併產生之商譽分配至預計將會受惠於合併之協同
cash-generating unit, or groups of cash generating units, that is       作用之各現金產生單位或現金產生單位之組別，
expected to benefit from the synergies of the combination and is        並於每年進行減值測試（見附註 1(k)(ii)）。
tested annually for impairment (see note 1(k)(ii)).

On disposal of a cash generating unit during the year, any              年內出售的現金產生單位，計算出售之損益時將
attributable amount of purchased goodwill is included in the            計入購入商譽之任何應佔金額。
calculation of the profit or loss on disposal.

–      Calculation of recoverable amount                                    –      計算可收回金額


       The recoverable amount of an asset is the greater of its fair               資產之可收回金額為其公允值減去出售成
       value less costs of disposal and value in use. In assessing                 本及使用價值兩者中之較高者。於評估使
       value in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted                用價值時，會使用除稅前折現率將估計未
       to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that                   來現金流量折現至其現值。該折現率反映
       reflects current market assessments of time value of money                  市場當時所評估之貨幣時間價值和該資產
       and the risks specific to the asset. Where an asset does not                之獨有風險。倘個別資產所產生之現金流
       generate cash inflows largely independent of those from                     入基本上不能獨立於其他資產所產生之現
       other assets, the recoverable amount is determined for                      金流入，則就獨立產生現金流入之最小資
       the smallest group of assets that generates cash inflows                    產組合（即現金產生單位）釐定可收回金
       independently (i.e. a cash-generating unit).                                額。


–      Recognition of impairment losses                                     –      確認減值虧損


       An impairment loss is recognised in profit or loss if the                   資產或其所屬之現金產生單位之賬面值高
       carrying amount of an asset, or the cash-generating unit                    於其可收回金額時，則會於損益確認減值
       to which it belongs, exceeds its recoverable amount.                        虧損。就現金產生單位確認之減值虧損會
       Impairment losses recognised in respect of cash-generating                  首先分配予削減已分配至該現金產生單位
       units are allocated first to reduce the carrying amount of any              （或一組單位）之任何商譽之賬面值，然後
       goodwill allocated to the cash-generating unit (or group of                 按比例削減該單位（或一組單位）內其他資
       units) and then, to reduce the carrying amount of the other                 產之賬面值，惟資產賬面值不可下調至低
       assets in the unit (or group of units) on a pro rata basis, except          於其個別公允值減去出售成本（如能計量）
       that the carrying value of an asset will not be reduced below               或使用價值（如能釐定）。
       its individual fair value less costs of disposal (if measurable),
       or value in use (if determinable).

       impairment loss in respect of goodwill is not reversed.

       carrying amount that would have been determined had no                      年度確認減值虧損而可釐定之資產賬面
       impairment loss been recognised in prior years. Reversals of                值。所撥回之減值虧損在確認撥回之年度

       which the reversals are recognised.

      recognised immediately in profit or loss.

      If a hedge of a forecast transaction subsequently results in the            若被對沖之預期交易其後導致確認非金融資產或
      recognition of a non-financial asset or non-financial liability, the        非金融負債，由權益重新分類之相關盈虧會計入
      associated gain or loss reclassified from equity is to be included in       該非金融資產或負債之最初成本或其他賬面值
      the initial cost or other carrying amount of the non-financial asset        內。
      or liability.

      If a hedge of a forecast transaction subsequently results in the            若被對沖之預期交易其後導致確認金融資產或金
      recognition of a financial asset or a financial liability, the associated   融負債，相關盈虧會在該購入之資產或承擔之負
      gain or loss is reclassified from equity to profit or loss in the same      債影響損益之同一個或多個期間內（例如當確認
      period or periods during which the asset acquired or liability              利息收入或支出時）由權益重新分類至損益。
      assumed affects profit or loss (such as when interest income or
      expense is recognised).

      For cash flow hedges, other than those covered by the preceding             有別於上述兩個政策所涵蓋之現金流量對沖，相
      two policy statements, the associated gain or loss is reclassified          關盈虧會在被對沖之預期交易影響損益之同一個
      from equity to profit or loss in the same period or periods during          或多個期間內由權益重新分類至損益。
      which the hedged forecast transaction affects profit or loss.

      When a hedging instrument expires or is sold, terminated                    當對沖工具到期或被出售、終止或行使或該實體
      or exercised, or the entity revokes designation of the hedge                取消該指定對沖關係，而被對沖之預期交易預期
      relationship but the hedged forecast transaction is still expected          仍會發生時，其累計盈虧會保留在權益內，直至
      to occur, the cumulative gain or loss at that point remains in equity       該交易發生為止，並按上述政策確認。若被對沖
      until the transaction occurs and it is recognised in accordance with        之交易預期不會發生，其累計未變現盈虧會即時
      the above policy. If the hedged transaction is no longer expected           由權益重新分類至損益。
      to take place, the cumulative unrealised gain or loss is reclassified
      from equity to profit or loss immediately.

      a straight-line basis over their estimated useful lives, after taking   估計剩餘價值（如有）後根據該等項目之估計可
      into account the estimated residual values, if any. The Group           使用年期以直線法計算。本集團至少每年審閱資
      reviews the estimated useful lives and the estimated residual           產之估計可使用年期及估計剩餘價值（如有），以
      values, if any, of the assets at least annually in order to determine   釐定在任何匯報日應被記錄之折舊費用數額。可
      the amount of depreciation expense to be recorded during any            使用年期及剩餘價值乃根據本集團以往在類似資
      reporting period. The useful lives and residual values are based        產上之經驗而釐定，並考慮到預期發生之技術變
      on the Group’s historical experience with similar assets and taking     化。倘若原來估計發生重大變化，則未來期間內
      into account anticipated technological changes. The depreciation        之折舊費用將被調整。
      expense for future periods is adjusted if there are significant
      changes from previous estimates.

(c) Write down of inventories                                          (c) 撇減存貨
The Group performs regular reviews of the carrying amounts             本集團參考存貨賬齡分析、預期未來耗用量及管
of inventories as disclosed in note 16 with reference to               理層之判斷，對存貨之賬面值進行定期審閱（於
aged inventories analysis, expected future consumption and             附註 16 披露）。倘存貨之估計可變現淨值跌至低
management judgement. Based on these reviews, write down of            於其賬面值，則本集團會根據審閱之結果而撇減
inventories will be made when the estimated net realisable value       存貨之價值。然而，實際耗用量可能與估計有所
of inventories decline below the carrying amount. However,             不同，而此估計之出入可能影響損益。
actual consumption may be different from estimation and profit
or loss could be affected by differences in this estimation.

(d) Deferred tax assets                                                (d) 遞延稅項資產
Deferred tax assets are recognised for unused tax losses and           遞延稅項資產乃就未動用稅務虧損及可抵扣暫時
temporary deductible differences. As those deferred tax assets         差額而確認。由於該等遞延稅項資產只限於有可
can only be recognised to the extent that it is probable that future   能使用未動用稅收抵免來抵銷日後應課稅溢利時
taxable profit will be available against which the unused tax          才會確認，因此需要管理層判斷日後獲得應課稅
benefits can be utilised, management’s judgements is required          溢利之可能性。本集團不斷審閱管理層之評估，
to assess the probability of future taxable profits. Management’s      倘未來應課稅溢利能使遞延稅項資產被收回，便
assessment is constantly reviewed and additional deferred tax          會確認額外之遞延稅項資產。遞延稅項資產之性
assets are recognised if it becomes probable that future taxable       質及賬面值詳情於附註 24(b) 披露。
profits will allow the deferred tax assets to be recovered. Details
of the nature and carrying amounts of deferred tax assets are
disclosed in note 24(b).

(e) Determining the lease term                                         (e) 釐定租賃期
As explained in policy note 1(j), the lease liability is initially     誠如附註 1(j) 政策所闡述，租賃負債按租賃期內
recognised at the present value of the lease payments payable          應付租賃付款之現值初步確認。於開始日期釐定
over the lease term. In determining the lease term at the              包含本集團可行使續租權之租賃租期時，本集團
commencement date for leases that include renewal options              會評估行使續租權之可能性，並考慮到所有能形
exercisable by the Group, the Group evaluates the likelihood of        成經濟誘因促使本集團行使續租權之相關事實及
exercising the renewal options taking into account all relevant        情況（包括有利條款、已進行之租賃裝修，以及
facts and circumstances that create an economic incentive for          該相關資產對本集團經營之重要性）。倘發生重
the Group to exercise the option, including favourable terms,          大事件或出現本集團控制範圍以內之重大變動情
leasehold improvements undertaken and the importance of that           況，則將重新評估租賃期。任何租賃期之延長或
underlying asset to the Group’s operation. The lease term is           縮短均會影響未來年度確認之租賃負債及使用權
reassessed when there is a significant event or significant change     資產金額。
in circumstance that is within the Group’s control. Any increase
or decrease in the lease term would affect the amount of lease
liabilities and right-of-use assets recognised in future years.

      The fair value measurement is categorised as a Level 3 valuation     公允值計量根據《香港財務報告準則》第 13 號
      under the three-level fair value hierarchy as defined in HKFRS       「公允值計量 」所界定之三級公允值架構分類為第
      13, Fair value measurement. The level into which a fair value        三級估值。將某公允值計量分類之等級乃經參考
      measurement is classified is determined with reference to            如下估值方法所用數據之可觀察性及重要性後釐
      observability and significance of the inputs used in the valuation   定：
      technique as follows:

      –     Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using only Level 1     –     第一級估值：僅使用第一級輸入數據（即於
            inputs i.e. unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for           計量日同類資產或負債於活躍市場之未經
            identical assets or liabilities at the measurement date;             調整報價）計量之公允值；


      –     Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using Level 2 inputs   –     第二級估值：使用第二級輸入數據（即未能
            i.e. observable inputs which fail to meet Level 1, and not           達到第一級之可觀察輸入數據）且並未使用
            using significant unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs           重大不可觀察輸入數據計量之公允值。不
            are inputs for which market data are not available; and              可觀察輸入數據為無市場數據之輸入數據；
                                                                                 及


      –     Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using significant      –     第三級估值：使用重大不可觀察輸入數據
            unobservable inputs.                                                 計量之公允值。

      of comparable properties and adjusted for building quality and       間作出調整。公允值計量之重大不可觀察輸入
      timing of the reference transaction. The significant unobservable    數據乃個別物業調整率，介乎 0.95 至 1.05（二零
      input in the fair value measurement is the property-specific         一九年：0.95 至 1.05）。公允值計量與個別物業
      adjusting rate, which ranged from 0.95 to 1.05 (2019: 0.95 to        調整率之間成正向關係。

(f) Capital management                                                 (f)   資本管理
The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital are to            本集團在管理資本時之首要目標為保障本集團能
safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern,          持續經營的能力，透過將產品及服務的價格訂於
so that it can continue to provide returns for shareholders and        與風險相稱的水平及按合理成本籌措融資，使其
benefits for other stakeholders, by pricing products and services      能繼續為股東提供回報及為其他利益相關人士提
commensurately with the level of risk and by securing access to        供利益。
finance at a reasonable cost.

The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital       本集團積極及定期對資本架構進行檢討及管理，
structure to maintain a balance between the higher shareholder         以在較高股東回報可能附帶較高借貸水平與雄厚
returns that might be possible with higher levels of borrowings        資本狀況帶來之優勢及保障間取得平衡，並因應
and the advantages and security afforded by a sound capital            經濟環境之變化對資本架構作出調整。
position, and makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
changes in economic conditions.

Consistent with industry practices, the Group monitors its capital     本著業內慣例，本集團以負債對經調整資本比率
structure on the basis of a debt-to-adjusted capital ratio. For this   作為監控其資本架構之基準。就此目的，本集團
purpose, the Group defines debt as the total of bank loans and         將負債界定為銀行貸款及租賃負債之總額，經調
lease liabilities, and adjusted capital as all components of equity    整資本為權益之所有組成部份減非累計擬派股
less unaccrued proposed dividends.                                     息。

      The Group has initially applied HKFRS 16 using the modified                      本集團已採用經修訂追溯法首次應用《香港財務
      retrospective approach. Under this approach, the Group                           報告準則》第 16 號。根據此方法，本集團就先前
      recognises right-of-use assets and corresponding lease liabilities               自二零一九年四月一日起入賬為經營租賃之絕大
      for almost all leases previously accounted for as operating leases               部分租賃確認使用權資產及相應租賃負債。此導
      as from 1st April 2019. This caused a significant increase in the                致本集團之負債總額明顯增加，及因此本集團於
      Group’s total debt and hence the Group’s debt-to-adjusted capital                二零一九年四月一日之負債對經調整資本比率與
      ratio rose from 1% to 10% on 1st April 2019 when compared to its                 於二零一九年三月三十一日之狀況相比由 1% 上
      position as at 31st March 2019.                                                  升至 10%。

      The Group’s strategy, which was unchanged from the year ended                    本集團之策略與截至二零一九年三月三十一日止
      31st March 2019, was to maintain the debt-to-adjusted capital                    年度保持不變，將負債對經調整資本比率維持於
      ratio below 30%. In order to maintain or adjust the ratio, the                   30% 以下。為保持或調整有關比率，本集團或會
      Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders,                   調整向股東派付之股息金額、發行新股份、向股
      issue new shares, return capital to shareholders, raise new debt                 東退回資本、籌集新債項融資或出售資產以減低

26 Financial risk management and fair                                                26 財務風險管理及金融工具之公
   values of financial instruments                                                      允值
Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risks                      本集團在一般業務過程中存在信貸、流動資金、
arises in the normal course of the Group’s business. The Group’s                     利率及貨幣風險。本集團面臨之有關風險及本集
exposures to these risks and the financial risk management                           團就管理該等風險所採用之財務風險管理政策與
policies and practices used by the Group to manage these risks                       慣例載述如下。
are described below.

(a) Credit risk                                                                      (a) 信貸風險
Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default on its               信貸風險指交易方違反其合約責任而導致本集團
contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to the Group.                  財務虧損之風險。


The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade and other                 本集團之信貸風險主要來自應收賬款及其他應收
receivables. Management has a credit policy in place and the                         款。管理層備有信貸政策，而所面臨之有關信貸
exposure to this credit risk is monitored on an ongoing basis.                       風險持續受到監控。


The Group’s cash and bank deposits are placed with financial                         本集團之現金及銀行存款乃存放於有良好信貸評
institutions with sound credit ratings, and the management                           級之金融機構，管理層認為本集團面臨之信貸風
consider the Group’s exposure to credit risk is low.                                 險為低。


The Group does not provide any guarantees which would expose                         本集團並無提供任何將令本集團面臨信貸風險之
the Group to credit risk.                                                            擔保。

      the Group’s financial performance. The Group will closely monitor     毒」之發展，並將持續評估其對本集團財務狀況
